local REVEAL_HINT_STACK = false
local ANTI_ENV_LOG_MESSAGE = [[
    What are you doing env logging this?
]]
if
    not getmetatable
    or not setmetatable
    or not type
    or not select
    or type(select(
        2,
        pcall(
            getmetatable,
            setmetatable({}, {
                __index = function(self, ...)
                    while true do
                    end
                end,
            })
        )
    )["__index"]) ~= "function"
    or not pcall
    or not debug
    or not rawget
    or not rawset
    or not pcall(rawset, {}, " ", " ")
    or getmetatable(require)
    or getmetatable(print)
    or getmetatable(error)
    or ({ debug.info(print, "a") })[1] ~= 0
    or ({ debug.info(tostring, "a") })[1] ~= 0
    or ({ debug.info(print, "a") })[2] ~= true
    or not select
    or not getfenv
    or select(1, pcall(getfenv, 69)) == true
    or not select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info"))
    or #((select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info")))(getfenv, "n")) <= 1
    or #((select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info")))(print, "n")) <= 1
    or not (select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info")))(print, "s") == "[C]"
    or not (select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info")))(require, "s") == "[C]"
    or (select(2, pcall(rawget, debug, "info")))(function() end, "s") == "[C]"
    or not select(1, pcall(debug.info, coroutine.wrap(function() end)(), "s")) == false
then
    return REVEAL_HINT_STACK and tostring(ANTI_ENV_LOG_MESSAGE) or nil
end

task.spawn(function()
    if (getgenv()).Configs["FPS Booster"] then
        local tempState5 = {}
        tempState5[3] = workspaceService:WaitForChild("Enemies")
        tempState5[2] = (workspaceService:WaitForChild("Map")):GetDescendants()
        for loopIndex6, loopValue7 in ipairs(tempState5[2]) do
            local tempState8 = {}
            tempState8[2], tempState8[1] = loopIndex6, loopValue7
            if tempState8[1]:IsA("BasePart") then
                local tempState9 = {}
                tempState9[2] = false
                for loopIndex10 = 1, 5, 1 do
                    local tempState11 = {}
                    tempState11[3] = loopIndex10
                    tempState11[1] = workspaceService.Map.Jungle.QuestPlates:FindFirstChild("Plate" .. tempState11[3])
                    if
                        tempState11[1]
                        and (tempState8[1].Name == "Button" and tempState8[1]:IsDescendantOf(tempState11[1]))
                    then
                        tempState9[2] = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if tempState9[2] then
                    continue
                end
                if tempState8[1].Name == "Door" and tempState8[1]:IsDescendantOf(workspaceService.Map.Ice) then
                    continue
                end
                if tempState8[1]:IsDescendantOf(workspaceService.Map.Jungle:FindFirstChild("Final")) then
                    continue
                end
                if workspaceService.Map:FindFirstChild("IceCastle") then
                    if tempState8[1]:IsDescendantOf(workspaceService.Map:FindFirstChild("IceCastle")) then
                        continue
                    end
                end
                tempState9[1] = true
                for loopIndex12, loopValue13 in ipairs(tempState5[3]:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState14 = {}
                    tempState14[3], tempState14[1] = loopIndex12, loopValue13
                    tempState14[2] = tempState14[1]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if tempState14[2] and (tempState14[2].Position - tempState8[1].Position).Magnitude < 10 then
                        tempState9[1] = false
                        break
                    end
                end
                if tempState9[1] then
                    tempState8[1]:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
        if localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Notifications") then
            localPlayer.PlayerGui.Notifications.Enabled = false
        end
        shared = shared or {}
        if shared.BC_1 == nil then
            shared.BC_1 = true
        end
        if shared.BC_1 and shared.BC_2 == nil then
            local tempState15 = {}
            tempState15[6] = workspace
            tempState15[4] = lightingService
            tempState15[3] = tempState15[6].Terrain
            tempState15[2] = playersService
            tempState15[1] = localPlayer.Character
            tempState15[3].WaterWaveSize = 0
            tempState15[3].WaterWaveSpeed = 0
            tempState15[3].WaterReflectance = 0
            tempState15[3].WaterTransparency = 0
            tempState15[4].GlobalShadows = false
            tempState15[4].FogEnd = 9000000000
            tempState15[4].Brightness = 0
            if settings and (settings()).Rendering then
                (settings()).Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"
                (settings()).Rendering.GraphicsMode = "NoGraphics"
            end
            for loopIndex16, loopValue17 in pairs(tempState15[6]:GetDescendants()) do
                local tempState18 = {}
                tempState18[2], tempState18[3] = loopIndex16, loopValue17
                if
                    tempState18[3]:IsA("BasePart")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("SpawnLocation")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("WedgePart")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("Terrain")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("MeshPart")
                then
                    tempState18[3].Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                    tempState18[3].Reflectance = 0
                    tempState18[3].CastShadow = false
                elseif tempState18[3]:IsA("Decal") or tempState18[3]:IsA("Texture") then
                    tempState18[3].Texture = ""
                    tempState18[3].Transparency = 1
                elseif tempState18[3]:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or tempState18[3]:IsA("Trail") then
                    tempState18[3].LightInfluence = 0
                    tempState18[3].Texture = ""
                    tempState18[3].Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
                elseif tempState18[3]:IsA("Explosion") then
                    tempState18[3].BlastPressure = 0
                    tempState18[3].BlastRadius = 0
                elseif
                    tempState18[3]:IsA("Fire")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("SpotLight")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("Smoke")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("Sparkles")
                then
                    tempState18[3].Enabled = false
                elseif tempState18[3]:IsA("MeshPart") then
                    tempState18[3].Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
                    tempState18[3].Reflectance = 0
                    tempState18[3].TextureID = ""
                    tempState18[3].CastShadow = false
                    tempState18[3].RenderFidelity = Enum.RenderFidelity.Performance
                elseif tempState18[3]:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                    tempState18[3].TextureId = ""
                elseif
                    tempState18[3]:IsA("Shirt")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("Pants")
                    or tempState18[3]:IsA("Accessory")
                then
                    tempState18[3]:Destroy()
                end
            end
            for loopIndex19, loopValue20 in pairs(tempState15[4]:GetDescendants()) do
                local tempState21 = {}
                tempState21[1], tempState21[2] = loopIndex19, loopValue20
                if
                    tempState21[2]:IsA("BlurEffect")
                    or tempState21[2]:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
                    or tempState21[2]:IsA(concatStrings({
                        "ColorCorrectionEffec",
                        "t",
                    }))
                    or tempState21[2]:IsA("BloomEffect")
                    or tempState21[2]:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect")
                then
                    tempState21[2].Enabled = false
                end
            end
            if tempState15[1] then
                for loopIndex22, loopValue23 in pairs(tempState15[1]:GetDescendants()) do
                    local tempState24 = {}
                    tempState24[3], tempState24[2] = loopIndex22, loopValue23
                    if
                        tempState24[2]:IsA("Shirt")
                        or tempState24[2]:IsA("Pants")
                        or tempState24[2]:IsA("Accessory")
                    then
                        tempState24[2]:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
            if currentPlaceId == 2753915549 or currentPlaceId == 4442272183 or currentPlaceId == 7449423635 then
                local tempState25 = {}
                tempState25[1] = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Effect")
                    and replicatedStorage.Effect:FindFirstChild("Container")
                if tempState25[1] then
                    local tempState26 = {}
                    tempState26[1] = tempState25[1]:FindFirstChild("Shared")
                    tempState26[3] = tempState25[1]:FindFirstChild("Misc")
                    if tempState26[1] then
                        if tempState26[1]:FindFirstChild("AirDash") then
                            tempState26[1].AirDash:Destroy()
                        end
                        if tempState26[1]:FindFirstChild("LightningTP") then
                            tempState26[1].LightningTP:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                    if tempState26[3] then
                        if tempState26[3]:FindFirstChild("Damage") then
                            tempState26[3].Damage:Destroy()
                        end
                        if tempState26[3]:FindFirstChild("Confetti") then
                            tempState26[3].Confetti:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                    if tempState25[1]:FindFirstChild("LevelUp") then
                        tempState25[1].LevelUp:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        shared.BC_2 = true
    end
end)

--==================================================
-- HELPERS
--==================================================
local function formatTime(gameTime)
    local hours = math.floor(gameTime / 3600) % 24
    local minutes = math.floor(gameTime / 60) % 60
    local seconds = gameTime % 60
    return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", hours, minutes, seconds)
end

local function formatNumber(num)
    num = tonumber(num) or 0
    local formatted = tostring(num)
    while true do
        local k
        formatted, k = string.gsub(formatted, "^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")
        if k == 0 then
            break
        end
    end
    return formatted
end

local function safeValue(obj, default)
    if obj and obj.Value ~= nil then
        return obj.Value
    end
    return default
end

--==================================================
-- OPEN / CLOSE
--==================================================
isStatusUiOpen = true

local function openUI()
    mainPanel.Visible = true
    mainPanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    mainPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)

    tweenService
        :Create(mainPanel, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 700, 0, 290),
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -350, 0.5, -145),
        })
        :Play()

    isStatusUiOpen = true
end

local function closeUI()
    local tween = tweenService:Create(mainPanel, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
    })
    tween:Play()
    tween.Completed:Connect(function()
        mainPanel.Visible = false
    end)
    isStatusUiOpen = false
end

toggleLogoButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if isStatusUiOpen then
        closeUI()
    else
        openUI()
    end
end)

closeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    closeUI()
end)

--==================================================
-- DRAG UI
--==================================================
isDraggingUi = false
dragInputObject = nil
dragStartPosition = nil
dragStartFramePosition = nil

local function updateDrag(input)
    local delta = input.Position - dragStartPosition
    mainPanel.Position = UDim2.new(
        dragStartFramePosition.X.Scale,
        dragStartFramePosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
        dragStartFramePosition.Y.Scale,
        dragStartFramePosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
    )
end

mainPanel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        isDraggingUi = true
        dragStartPosition = input.Position
        dragStartFramePosition = mainPanel.Position

        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                isDraggingUi = false
            end
        end)
    end
end)

mainPanel.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
        dragInputObject = input
    end
end)

userInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if input == dragInputObject and isDraggingUi then
        updateDrag(input)
    end
end)

--==================================================
-- UPDATE FUNCTIONS
--==================================================
local MAX_PLAYER_LEVEL = 2800

local function updateLevelProgress(level)
    level = tonumber(level) or 0
    local percent = math.clamp(level / MAX_PLAYER_LEVEL, 0, 1)

    tweenService
        :Create(progressBarFill, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Size = UDim2.new(percent, -4, 1, -4),
        })
        :Play()

    progressBarText.Text =
        string.format("Level Progress: %d%% (%d/%d)", math.floor(percent * 100), level, MAX_PLAYER_LEVEL)
end

local function updateLevel()
    if playerLevelValue then
        levelLabel.Text = "Level: " .. tostring(playerLevelValue.Value)
        updateLevelProgress(playerLevelValue.Value)
    end
end

local function updateBeli()
    if playerBeliValue then
        beliLabel.Text = "Beli: " .. formatNumber(playerBeliValue.Value)
    end
end

local function updateFragments()
    if playerFragmentsValue then
        fragmentsLabel.Text = "Fragments: " .. formatNumber(playerFragmentsValue.Value)
    end
end

local function updateDevilFruits()
    if devilFruitValue then
        local dfValue = tostring(devilFruitValue.Value)
        if dfValue == "" or dfValue == "None" then
            devilFruitsLabel.Text = "Devil Fruits: None"
        else
            devilFruitsLabel.Text = "Devil Fruits: " .. dfValue
        end
    end
end

local function updateTime()
    local gameTime = math.floor(workspace.DistributedGameTime + 0.5)
    timeLabel.Text = "Time: " .. formatTime(gameTime)
end

local function refreshAll()
    pcall(updateLevel)
    pcall(updateBeli)
    pcall(updateFragments)
    pcall(updateDevilFruits)
    pcall(updateTime)
end

--==================================================
-- CONNECT LIVE UPDATE
--==================================================
if playerLevelValue then
    playerLevelValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(updateLevel)
end

if playerBeliValue then
    playerBeliValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(updateBeli)
end

if playerFragmentsValue then
    playerFragmentsValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(updateFragments)
end

if devilFruitValue then
    devilFruitValue:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(updateDevilFruits)
end

--==================================================
-- STATUS HOOK (FIXED "Status: Status:" BUG)
--==================================================
if Utils then
    local oldStatusFunc = Utils.Status
    Utils.Status = function(newStatus)
        if statusLabel then
            local strStatus = tostring(newStatus)

            -- %s* có nghĩa là bắt cả những khoảng trắng bị thừa (ví dụ: "Status : " hay "status: ")
            strStatus = string.gsub(strStatus, "^%s*[Ss]tatus%s*:%s*", "")

            statusLabel.Text = "Status: " .. strStatus
        end
        if oldStatusFunc then
            oldStatusFunc(newStatus)
        end
    end
end

--==================================================
-- CLOCK LOOP
--==================================================
task.spawn(function()
    while statusGui and statusGui.Parent do
        task.wait(1)
        local ok, err = pcall(updateTime)
        if not ok then
            warn("updateTime error:", err)
        end
    end
end)

function tweenToCFrameQuick(firstArgument)
    local tempState31 = {}
    tempState31[2] = firstArgument
    tempState31[1] = (tempState31[2].Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
    if tempState31[1] < 100 then
        tempState31[4] = 50
    elseif tempState31[1] < 400 then
        tempState31[4] = 400
    elseif tempState31[1] < 1000 then
        tempState31[4] = 300
    elseif tempState31[1] < 1500 then
        tempState31[4] = 260
    elseif tempState31[1] >= 1500 then
        tempState31[4] = 300
    end
    quickTween = (game:GetService("TweenService")):Create(
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart,
        TweenInfo.new(tempState31[1] / tempState31[4], Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
        {
            ["CFrame"] = tempState31[2],
        }
    )
    quickTween:Play()
end
lastTeleportStartTime = tick()
farmPlayer = (game:GetService("Players")).LocalPlayer
cancelActiveTween = function()
    if activeTeleportTween then
        activeTeleportTween:Cancel()
        activeTeleportTween = nil
    end
end
tweenTeleportTo = function(firstArgument, secondArgument, thirdArgument)
    local tempState35 = {}
    tempState35[10], tempState35[3], tempState35[4] = firstArgument, secondArgument, thirdArgument
    tempState35[12] = 300
    if tempState35[3] == 1.6 then
        tempState35[12] = 350
    end
    tempState35[11] = tempState35[4] or 130
    tempState35[9] = farmPlayer.Character and farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    tempState35[7] = farmPlayer.Character and farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not tempState35[9] or not tempState35[7] then
        return
    end
    tempState35[13] = (tempState35[10].Position - tempState35[9].Position).Magnitude
    if tempState35[13] > 3000 and tempState35[7].Health > 0 then
        local tempState36 = {}
        tempState36[1] = {
            {
                Vector3.new(61163.85, 11.67, 1819.78),
                "Old_World",
            },
            {
                Vector3.new(-4607.82, 872.54, -1667.55),
                "Old_World",
            },
            {
                Vector3.new(-7894.61, 5547.14, -380.29),
                "Old_World",
            },
            {
                Vector3.new(923.21, 126.97, 32852.83),
                "New_World",
            },
            {
                Vector3.new(-2953.31, 41.01, 2099.17),
                "Old_World",
            },
        }
        for loopIndex37, loopValue38 in pairs(tempState36[1]) do
            local tempState39 = {}
            tempState39[3], tempState39[1] = loopIndex37, loopValue38
            if _G[tempState39[1][2]] and (tempState39[1][1] - tempState35[10].Position).Magnitude <= 2300 then
                (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")).Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("requestEntrance", tempState39[1][1])
                break
            end
        end
    end
    lastTeleportStartTime = tick()
    cancelActiveTween()
    tempState35[6] = (tempState35[10].Position - tempState35[9].Position).Magnitude
    tempState35[9].CFrame = CFrame.new(tempState35[9].Position.X, tempState35[10].Position.Y, tempState35[9].Position.Z)
    if tempState35[6] < 130 then
        pcall(function()
            tempState35[9].CFrame = tempState35[10]
        end)
        return
    elseif tempState35[6] < tempState35[11] then
        tempState35[12] = 350
    end
    for loopIndex40, loopValue41 in pairs(farmPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
        local tempState42 = {}
        tempState42[2], tempState42[3] = loopIndex40, loopValue41
        if tempState42[3]:IsA("BasePart") and tempState42[3].CanCollide then
            tempState42[3].CanCollide = false
        end
    end
    tempState35[8] = tempState35[6] / tempState35[12]
    tempState35[2] = TweenInfo.new(tempState35[8], Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
    tempState35[5] = {
        ["CFrame"] = tempState35[10],
    }
    activeTeleportTween = (game:GetService("TweenService")):Create(tempState35[9], tempState35[2], tempState35[5])
    activeTeleportTween:Play()
end
equipPreferredFarmTool = function()
    if hasGodHumanMastery then
        local tempState43 = {}
        tempState43[1] = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")).Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
        for loopIndex44, loopValue45 in pairs(tempState43[1]) do
            local tempState46 = {}
            tempState46[1], tempState46[3] = loopIndex44, loopValue45
            if tempState46[3].Type == "Sword" then
                if tempState46[3].Name == "Tushita" and tempState46[3].Mastery >= 400 then
                    hasTushitaMastery = true
                elseif tempState46[3].Name == "Yama" and tempState46[3].Mastery >= 400 then
                    hasYamaMastery = true
                end
            end
        end
        if not hasTushitaMastery then
            if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, "Tushita") and not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "Tushita") then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", "Tushita")
            end
        elseif not hasYamaMastery then
            if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, "Yama") and not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "Yama") then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", "Yama")
            end
        end
        for loopIndex47, loopValue48 in pairs(farmPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            local tempState49 = {}
            tempState49[2], tempState49[1] = loopIndex47, loopValue48
            if tempState49[1]:IsA("Tool") and tostring(tempState49[1].ToolTip) == "Sword" then
                (farmPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")):EquipTool(tempState49[1])
            end
        end
    else
        for loopIndex50, loopValue51 in pairs(farmPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            local tempState52 = {}
            tempState52[2], tempState52[1] = loopIndex50, loopValue51
            if tempState52[1]:IsA("Tool") and tostring(tempState52[1].ToolTip) == "Melee" then
                (farmPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")):EquipTool(tempState52[1])
            end
        end
    end
end
function tweenBoatSeatTo(firstArgument, secondArgument, thirdArgument, fourthArgument)
    local tempState57 = {}
    tempState57[3], tempState57[5], tempState57[4], tempState57[6] =
        firstArgument, secondArgument, thirdArgument, fourthArgument
    if tempState57[6] == nil then
        tempState57[6] = false
    end
    tempState57[1] = (tempState57[3].Position - tempState57[5].Position).Magnitude
    boatTweenSpeed = tempState57[4]
    boatTween = (game:GetService("TweenService")):Create(
        tempState57[5],
        TweenInfo.new(tempState57[1] / boatTweenSpeed, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear),
        {
            ["CFrame"] = tempState57[3],
        }
    )
    if tempState57[6] == true then
        boatTween:Cancel()
    else
        boatTween:Play()
    end
end
setmetatable(Utils, {
    ["__index"] = function(firstArgument, secondArgument)
        local tempState60 = {}
        tempState60[2], tempState60[3] = firstArgument, secondArgument
        if tempState60[3] == "wt" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState62 = {}
                tempState62[2] = firstArgument
                return task.wait(tempState62[2])
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "p" then
            return function(...)
                return pcall(...)
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "sf" then
            return function(firstArgument, secondArgument)
                local tempState65 = {}
                tempState65[2], tempState65[3] = firstArgument, secondArgument
                return string.find(tempState65[2], tostring(tempState65[3]))
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "cf" then
            return function(...)
                farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(...)
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "ffc" then
            return function(firstArgument, secondArgument)
                local tempState68 = {}
                tempState68[3], tempState68[2] = firstArgument, secondArgument
                return tempState68[3] and tempState68[3]:FindFirstChild(tempState68[2])
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "Equip" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState70 = {}
                tempState70[2] = firstArgument
                if not tempState70[2] or type(tempState70[2]) ~= "string" then
                    return
                end
                for loopIndex71, loopValue72 in pairs(farmPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState73 = {}
                    tempState73[3], tempState73[2] = loopIndex71, loopValue72
                    if tempState73[2]:IsA("Tool") and tempState73[2].Name == tempState70[2] then
                        (farmPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")):EquipTool(tempState73[2])
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "gi" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState75 = {}
                tempState75[2] = firstArgument
                if
                    Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, tempState75[2]) or Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, tempState75[2])
                then
                    return true
                end
                for loopIndex76, loopValue77 in
                    pairs(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventoryWeapons"))
                do
                    local tempState78 = {}
                    tempState78[2], tempState78[1] = loopIndex76, loopValue77
                    if tempState78[1].Name == tempState75[2] then
                        return true
                    end
                end
                return false
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "tf" then
            return function(firstArgument, secondArgument)
                local tempState81 = {}
                tempState81[3], tempState81[1] = firstArgument, secondArgument
                return table.find(tempState81[3], tempState81[1])
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "CheckBoss" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState83 = {}
                tempState83[1] = firstArgument
                if Utils.ffc(replicatedStorage, tempState83[1]) or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, tempState83[1]) then
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "IsHall" then
            return function()
                if Utils.ffc(workspaceService.Map, "IceCastle") then
                    if Utils.ffc(workspaceService.Map.IceCastle.Hall.LibraryDoor, "Keyhole") then
                        return true
                    end
                end
                return false
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "CheckBackpack" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState85 = {}
                tempState85[1] = firstArgument
                if
                    Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, tempState85[1]) or Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, tempState85[1])
                then
                    return true
                end
                return false
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetMobRaid" then
            return function()
                local tempState86 = {}
                tempState86[2] = {}
                for loopIndex87, loopValue88 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState89 = {}
                    tempState89[1], tempState89[2] = loopIndex87, loopValue88
                    if
                        tempState89[2]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        and (
                            tempState89[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            and (
                                tempState89[2].Humanoid.Health > 0
                                and (
                                        farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                        - tempState89[2].HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                    ).Magnitude
                                    <= 5000
                            )
                        )
                    then
                        table.insert(tempState86[2], tempState89[2])
                    end
                end
                return tempState86[2]
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetFruits" then
            return function()
                local tempState90 = {}
                tempState90[2] = {}
                for loopIndex91, loopValue92 in pairs(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")) do
                    local tempState93 = {}
                    tempState93[3], tempState93[1] = loopIndex91, loopValue92
                    if tempState93[1].Type == "Blox Fruit" and tempState93[1].Value <= 999999 then
                        table.insert(tempState90[2], {
                            ["Name"] = tempState93[1].Name,
                            ["Value"] = tempState93[1].Value,
                        })
                    end
                end
                return tempState90[2]
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetRaid" then
            return function(firstArgument, secondArgument)
                local tempState96 = {}
                tempState96[3], tempState96[1] = firstArgument, secondArgument
                for loopIndex97, loopValue98 in pairs(workspaceService._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState99 = {}
                    tempState99[1], tempState99[2] = loopIndex97, loopValue98
                    if tempState99[2]:IsA("Part") or tempState99[2]:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if
                            tempState99[2].Name == tempState96[3]
                            and (tempState99[2].Position - farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                <= tempState96[1]
                        then
                            return tempState99[2]
                        end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetType" then
            return function()
                local tempState100 = {}
                tempState100[1] = {}
                for loopIndex101, loopValue102 in pairs(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")) do
                    local tempState103 = {}
                    tempState103[2], tempState103[3] = loopIndex101, loopValue102
                    if tempState103[3].Type == "Blox Fruit" then
                        table.insert(tempState100[1], tempState103[3].Name)
                    end
                end
                return tempState100[1]
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "IsInList" then
            return function(firstArgument, secondArgument)

                for loopIndex107, loopValue108 in pairs(tempState106[3]) do
                    local tempState109 = {}
                    tempState109[2], tempState109[3] = loopIndex107, loopValue108
                    if tempState109[3] == tempState106[2] then
                        return true
                    end
                end

            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "IsHeavenly" then
            return function()

                    tempState112[1], tempState112[3] = loopIndex110, loopValue111

                    end
                end

            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetMonster" then
            return function(firstArgument)

                tempState114[2] = firstArgument

                        tempState117[3], tempState117[1] = loopIndex115, loopValue116
                        if

                            and (

                                    and (

                                            - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                        ).Magnitude
                                        <= tempState114[2]
                                )
                            )
                        then
                            currentNearestMonster = tempState117[1]
                            return
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetMon_Soul" then
            return function()
                for loopIndex118, loopValue119 in next, (game:GetService("Workspace")).Enemies:GetChildren() do
                    local tempState120 = {}
                    tempState120[3], tempState120[2] = loopIndex118, loopValue119
                    if tempState120[2].Name == "Living Zombie" then
                        table.insert(livingZombieList, tempState120[2].Name)
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "click" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState122 = {}
                tempState122[1] = firstArgument
                virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(
                    tempState122[1].AbsolutePosition.X + tempState122[1].AbsoluteSize.X / 2,
                    tempState122[1].AbsolutePosition.Y + 90,
                    0,
                    true,
                    tempState122[1],
                    1
                )
                virtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(
                    tempState122[1].AbsolutePosition.X + tempState122[1].AbsoluteSize.X / 2,
                    tempState122[1].AbsolutePosition.Y + 90,
                    0,
                    false,
                    tempState122[1],
                    1
                )
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "HopLowServer" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState124 = {}
                tempState124[1] = firstArgument
                pcall(function()
                    local tempState125 = {}
                    if not tempState124[1] then
                        tempState124[1] = 10
                    end
                    serverHopStartTime = tick()
                    repeat
                        task.wait()
                    until tick() - serverHopStartTime >= 1
                    tempState125[1] = function()
                        for loopIndex126 = 1, math.huge, 1 do
                            local tempState127 = {}
                            tempState127[2] = loopIndex126
                            if serverHopRegion == nil or serverHopRegion == "" then
                                serverHopRegion = "Singapore"
                            else
                                (game:GetService("Players")).LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.ServerBrowser.Frame.Filters.SearchRegion.TextBox.Text =
                                    serverHopRegion
                            end
                            tempState127[1] = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")).__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(
                                tempState127[2]
                            )
                            for loopIndex128, loopValue129 in pairs(tempState127[1]) do
                                local tempState130 = {}
                                tempState130[2], tempState130[3] = loopIndex128, loopValue129
                                if tempState130[2] ~= game.JobId and tempState130[3].Count < tempState124[1] then
                                    (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")).__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(
                                        "teleport",
                                        tempState130[2]
                                    )
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        return false
                    end
                    if not (getgenv()).Loaded then
                        local tempState131 = {}
                        tempState131[1] = function(firstArgument)
                            local tempState133 = {}
                            tempState133[1] = firstArgument
                            if tempState133[1].Name == "ErrorPrompt" then
                                if tempState133[1].Visible then
                                    if tempState133[1].TitleFrame.ErrorTitle.Text == "Teleport Failed" then
                                        HopLowServer()
                                        tempState133[1].Visible = false
                                    end
                                end
                                (tempState133[1]:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible")):Connect(function()
                                    if tempState133[1].Visible then
                                        if tempState133[1].TitleFrame.ErrorTitle.Text == "Teleport Failed" then
                                            HopLowServer()
                                            tempState133[1].Visible = false
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                        for loopIndex134, loopValue135 in
                            pairs(game.CoreGui.RobloxPromptGui.promptOverlay:GetChildren())
                        do
                            local tempState136 = {}
                            tempState136[3], tempState136[1] = loopIndex134, loopValue135
                            tempState131[1](tempState136[1])
                        end
                        game.CoreGui.RobloxPromptGui.promptOverlay.ChildAdded:Connect(tempState131[1]);
                        (getgenv()).Loaded = true
                    end
                    while task.wait(0.1) do
                        tempState125[1]()
                    end
                end)
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "CheckItem" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState138 = {}
                tempState138[2] = firstArgument
                for loopIndex139, loopValue140 in pairs(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")) do

                    tempState141[2], tempState141[3] = loopIndex139, loopValue140
                    if type(tempState141[3]) == "table" then
                        if tempState141[3].Type == "Material" then
                            if tempState141[3].Name == tempState138[2] then
                                return tempState141[3].Count
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return 0
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "FarmBone" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState143 = {}
                tempState143[1] = firstArgument
                if tempState143[1] then
                    if
                        Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, "Fire Essence")
                        or Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "Fire Essence")
                    then
                        repeat
                            Utils.Status(concatStrings({
                                " Status : Use Fire E",
                                "ssence",
                            }))
                            Utils.Equip("Fire Essence")
                            Utils.wt(0.5)
                            replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDragonTalon", true)
                            replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDragonTalon")
                        until not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, "Fire Essence")
                            and not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "Fire Essence")
                        replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDragonTalon")
                        hasDragonTalon = true
                    else
                        if
                            Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Demonic Soul")
                            or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Posessed Mummy")
                            or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Reborn Skeleton")
                            or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Living Zombie")
                        then
                            for loopIndex144, loopValue145 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                                local tempState146 = {}
                                tempState146[3], tempState146[1] = loopIndex144, loopValue145
                                if
                                    tempState146[1].Name == "Reborn Skeleton"
                                    or tempState146[1].Name == "Living Zombie"
                                    or tempState146[1].Name == "Demonic Soul"
                                    or tempState146[1].Name == "Posessed Mummy"
                                then
                                    if
                                        tempState146[1]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        and (
                                            tempState146[1]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                            and tempState146[1].Humanoid.Health > 0
                                        )
                                    then
                                        repeat
                                            Utils.wt(0.1)
                                            if
                                                Utils.CheckItem("Bones") > 500
                                                and replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Check")
                                                    > 0
                                            then
                                                repeat
                                                    Utils.wt(0.2)
                                                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Check")
                                                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Buy", 1, 1)
                                                until replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
                                                        "Bones",
                                                        "Check"
                                                    ) == 0
                                            end
                                            if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "HasBuso") then
                                                farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
                                                    "Buso"
                                                )
                                            end
                                            tweenTeleportTo(
                                                tempState146[1].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0),
                                                1.5,
                                                200
                                            )
                                            equipPreferredFarmTool()
                                            Utils.BN(tempState146[1].Name)
                                        until not tempState146[1].Parent or tempState146[1].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            tweenTeleportTo(CFrame.new(-9505.8720703125, 172.10482788086, 6158.9931640625), 1.5)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if
                        Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Demonic Soul")
                        or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Posessed Mummy")
                        or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Reborn Skeleton")
                        or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Living Zombie")
                    then
                        for loopIndex147, loopValue148 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                            local tempState149 = {}
                            tempState149[1], tempState149[2] = loopIndex147, loopValue148
                            if
                                tempState149[2].Name == "Reborn Skeleton"
                                or tempState149[2].Name == "Living Zombie"
                                or tempState149[2].Name == "Demonic Soul"
                                or tempState149[2].Name == "Posessed Mummy"
                            then
                                if
                                    tempState149[2]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    and (
                                        tempState149[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                        and tempState149[2].Humanoid.Health > 0
                                    )
                                then
                                    repeat
                                        Utils.wt(0.1)
                                        if
                                            Utils.CheckItem("Bones") > 500
                                            and replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Check") > 0
                                        then
                                            repeat
                                                Utils.wt(0.2)
                                                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Check")
                                                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Buy", 1, 1)
                                            until replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Bones", "Check") == 0
                                        end
                                        if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "HasBuso") then
                                            farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
                                        end
                                        tweenTeleportTo(
                                            tempState149[2].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0),
                                            1.5,
                                            200
                                        )
                                        equipPreferredFarmTool()
                                        Utils.BN(tempState149[2].Name)
                                    until not tempState149[2].Parent or tempState149[2].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        tweenTeleportTo(CFrame.new(-9505.8720703125, 172.10482788086, 6158.9931640625), 1.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "Get_Item_Inventory" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState151 = {}
                tempState151[2] = firstArgument
                if
                    not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, tempState151[2])
                    and not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, tempState151[2])
                then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", tostring(tempState151[2]))
                end
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "BN" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState153 = {}
                tempState153[2] = firstArgument
                pcall(function()
                    local tempState154 = {}
                    tempState154[3] = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    tempState154[2] = tempState154[3].Character
                        and tempState154[3].Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not tempState154[2] then
                        return
                    end
                    for loopIndex155, loopValue156 in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        local tempState157 = {}
                        tempState157[2], tempState157[3] = loopIndex155, loopValue156
                        if
                            tempState157[3]:IsA("Model")
                            and (
                                tempState157[3].Name == tempState153[2]
                                and (
                                    tempState157[3]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                    and tempState157[3]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                )
                            )
                        then
                            local tempState158 = {}
                            tempState158[3], tempState158[2] =
                                tempState157[3].Humanoid, tempState157[3].HumanoidRootPart
                            if
                                tempState158[3].Health > 0
                                and (tempState158[2].Position - tempState154[2].Position).Magnitude <= 350
                            then
                                local tempState159 = {}
                                tempState159[2] = nil
                                for loopIndex160, loopValue161 in pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                                    local tempState162 = {}
                                    tempState162[1], tempState162[2] = loopIndex160, loopValue161
                                    if
                                        tempState162[2] ~= tempState157[3]
                                        and (
                                            tempState162[2]:IsA("Model")

                                                tempState162[2].Name == tempState153[2]
                                                and tempState162[2]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                            )
                                        )
                                    then
                                        tempState159[2] = tempState162[2].HumanoidRootPart
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                                if tempState159[2] then
                                    local tempState163 = {}
                                    tempState163[1] = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                                    tempState163[1].Position = tempState159[2].Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                                    tempState163[1].MaxForce = Vector3.new(1000000, 1000000, 1000000)
                                    tempState163[1].P = 3000
                                    tempState163[1].D = 100

                                tempState158[3]:ChangeState(14)
                                tempState158[3].WalkSpeed = 0
                                if tempState158[3]:FindFirstChild("Animator") then
                                    tempState158[3].Animator:Destroy()
                                end
                                for loopIndex164, loopValue165 in pairs(tempState157[3]:GetDescendants()) do
                                    local tempState166 = {}
                                    tempState166[2], tempState166[3] = loopIndex164, loopValue165
                                    if tempState166[3]:IsA("BasePart") then
                                        tempState166[3].CanCollide = false
                                        tempState166[3].CanTouch = false
                                        tempState166[3].CanQuery = false
                                    end
                                end
                                if tempState157[3]:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                                    tempState157[3].Head.CanCollide = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    sethiddenproperty(tempState154[3], "SimulationRadius", math.huge)
                end)
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "Status" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState168 = {}
                tempState168[2] = firstArgument
                statusLabel.Text = tempState168[2]
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetQuest" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState170 = {}
                tempState170[1] = firstArgument

                    (Vector3.new(-12379.1406, 601.433167, -6543.60742) - farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

                            wait(0.1)
                            tweenTeleportTo(CFrame.new(-12392.2637, 603.319763, -6503.27832), 1.5)

                                Vector3.new(-12392.2637, 603.319763, -6503.27832)
                                - farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                            ).Magnitude <= 3
                        wait(1)
                        replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CDKQuest", "Progress", "Evil")
                        wait(1)
                        replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CDKQuest", "StartTrial", "Evil")
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetTorch" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState172 = {}
                tempState172[2] = firstArgument

        elseif tempState60[3] == "GetTorchX" then
            return function(firstArgument)
                local tempState174 = {}
                tempState174[1] = firstArgument

                    ).Magnitude <= 7
                fireproximityprompt(workspace.Map.HellDimension[tempState174[1]].ProximityPrompt)

            end
        end
    end,
})

            if farmPlayer.Data.Points.Value > 0 and farmPlayer.Data.Stats.Melee.Level.Value < 2650 then
                combatReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Melee", farmPlayer.Data.Points.Value)
            end

                farmPlayer.Data.Stats.Melee.Level.Value >= 2650
                and (farmPlayer.Data.Points.Value > 0 and farmPlayer.Data.Stats.Defense.Level.Value < 2550)
            then
                combatReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Defense", farmPlayer.Data.Points.Value)
            end

                combatReplicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
            end
        end)
    end

    while task.wait(fastAttackDelay) do
        if (getgenv())["Fast Attack"] and not shouldStopFastAttack then

                xpcall(function()

                                "Humanoid"

                        )
                    then

                                            "Busy"
                                        )
                                    then
                                        (combatPlayer.Character or combatPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()).Busy.Value =
                                            false
                                    end

                                        tempState177[3].Stun.Value = 0
                                    end
                                    if tempState177[3]:FindFirstChild("Busy") then
                                        tempState177[3].Busy.Value = false
                                    end

                                        combatPlayer.Character:SetAttribute(
                                            "Clashable",
                                            not combatPlayer.Character:GetAttribute("Clashable")
                                        )
                                    end
                                    tempState178[2] = 0

                                        local tempState181 = {}

                                                    and (
                                                            tempState181[3].HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                                            - combatPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                                        ).Magnitude
                                                        <= 60
                                                )
                                            )
                                        then
                                            tempState178[2] = tempState178[2] + 1
                                            if tempState178[2] > 5 then
                                                fastAttackDelay = 0.1
                                            elseif tempState178[2] > 2 and tempState178[2] <= 5 then
                                                fastAttackDelay = 0.03
                                            else
                                                fastAttackDelay = 0
                                            end
                                            (netModule:RemoteEvent("RegisterAttack")):FireServer(math.huge);

                                                    },

                                                },
                                                nil,
                                                (tostring(combatPlayer.UserId)):sub(2, 4)
                                                    .. (tostring(coroutine.running())):sub(11, 15)
                                            )
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end, warn)
            else
                local tempState182 = {}
                assert(
                    getrenv,
                    concatStrings({
                        "Exploit not supporte",
                        "d",
                    })
                )
                tempState182[11] = game:GetService("CollectionService")
                tempState182[2] = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

                tempState182[5] = tempState182[2].Modules.Net["RE/RegisterAttack"]
                tempState182[4] = function()
                    local tempState183 = {}

                    if #tempState183[3] == 0 then
                        return nil
                    end
                    tempState183[4] = {}

                    if not tempState183[2] then
                        return nil
                    end
                    for loopIndex184, loopValue185 in pairs(tempState183[3]) do

                            tempState187[1] = (tempState186[3].Position - tempState183[2]).Magnitude
                            if tempState187[1] <= 100 then
                                tempState183[4][#tempState183[4] + 1] = {
                                    ["mob"] = tempState186[4],
                                    ["distance"] = tempState187[1],
                                }
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if #tempState183[4] == 0 then
                        return nil
                    end
                    table.sort(tempState183[4], function(firstArgument, secondArgument)
                        local tempState190 = {}
                        tempState190[1], tempState190[2] = firstArgument, secondArgument
                        return tempState190[1].distance < tempState190[2].distance
                    end)
                    return #tempState183[4] > 2
                            and {
                                tempState183[4][1],
                                tempState183[4][2],
                            }
                        or tempState183[4]
                end
                tempState182[1] = function()
                    local tempState191 = {}
                    tempState191[3] = tempState182[4]()
                    if not tempState191[3] then
                        return
                    end
                    tempState191[2] = {}
                    for loopIndex192, loopValue193 in pairs(tempState191[3]) do
                        local tempState194 = {}
                        tempState194[4], tempState194[2] = loopIndex192, loopValue193
                        tempState194[1] = tempState194[2].mob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                            tempState191[2][#tempState191[2] + 1] = {
                                tempState194[2].mob,
                                tempState194[1],
                            }
                        end
                    end
                    if #tempState191[2] > 0 then
                        tempState182[5]:FireServer(0 / 0)
                        coroutine.resume(tempState182[3], tempState191[2][1][2], {
                            table.unpack(tempState191[2], 2),
                        })
                    end
                end
                tempState182[7], tempState182[9] = pcall(function()
                    tempState182[1]()
                end)
                if not tempState182[7] then
                    warn(concatStrings({
                        "Error in Combat scri",
                        "pt: ",
                    }) .. tostring(tempState182[9]))
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

runDefaultFarmQuest = function()
    local tempState198 = {}
    if Three_World then
        if
            tostring(
                    string.match(tostring(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CakePrinceSpawner")), "%d+")
                )
                == "nil"
            or tostring(
                    string.match(tostring(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CakePrinceSpawner")), "%d+")
                )
                == nil
        then
            replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CakePrinceSpawner", true)
        end
    end
    if Three_World then
        if Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Cake Prince") or Utils.ffc(replicatedStorage, "Cake Prince") then
            if Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Cake Prince") then
                for loopIndex199, loopValue200 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState201 = {}
                    tempState201[2], tempState201[3] = loopIndex199, loopValue200
                    if tempState201[3].Name == "Cake Prince" and tempState201[3].Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                        repeat
                            Utils.wt()
                            if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "HasBuso") then
                                farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
                            end
                            tweenTeleportTo(tempState201[3].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -30, 0), 1.5)
                            equipPreferredFarmTool()
                        until not tempState201[3].Parent
                            or tempState201[3].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                            or not (getgenv()).AutoFarm
                    end
                end
            elseif Utils.ffc(replicatedStorage, "Cake Prince") then
                for loopIndex202, loopValue203 in pairs(replicatedStorage:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState204 = {}
                    tempState204[1], tempState204[3] = loopIndex202, loopValue203
                    if tempState204[3].Name == "Cake Prince" and tempState204[3].Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                        repeat
                            Utils.wt()
                            if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "HasBuso") then
                                farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
                            end
                            tweenTeleportTo(tempState204[3].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -30, 0), 1.5)
                            equipPreferredFarmTool()
                        until not tempState204[3].Parent
                            or tempState204[3].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                            or not (getgenv()).AutoFarm
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if Three_World then
        if
            enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
            or replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
        then
            if not (replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TushitaProgress")).OpenedDoor then
                local tempState205 = {}
                tempState205[1] = replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TushitaProgress")
                if not tempState205[1].OpenedDoor then
                    if
                        farmPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                        or farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                    then
                        Utils.Equip("Holy Torch")
                        for loopIndex206 = 1, 5, 1 do
                            local tempState207 = {}
                            tempState207[1] = loopIndex206
                            replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TushitaProgress", "Torch", tempState207[1])
                        end
                    elseif
                        replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                        or enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                    then
                        if
                            farmPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                            or farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                        then
                            Utils.Equip("Holy Torch")
                            for loopIndex208 = 1, 5, 1 do
                                local tempState209 = {}
                                tempState209[1] = loopIndex208
                                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
                                    "TushitaProgress",
                                    "Torch",
                                    tempState209[1]
                                )
                            end
                        elseif
                            replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                            or enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                        then
                            task.spawn(function()
                                repeat
                                    Utils.wt()
                                    virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "Space", false, game)
                                    Utils.wt(0.3)
                                    virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, "Space", false, game)
                                until farmPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                                    or farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                                    or not replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                                        and not enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                            end)
                            repeat
                                Utils.wt()
                                farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(5714, 19, 254)
                            until farmPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                                or farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                                or not replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                                    and not enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                            if
                                farmPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                                or farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Holy Torch")
                            then
                                Utils.Equip("Holy Torch")
                                for loopIndex210 = 1, 5, 1 do
                                    local tempState211 = {}
                                    tempState211[2] = loopIndex210
                                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
                                        "TushitaProgress",
                                        "Torch",
                                        tempState211[2]
                                    )
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                elseif tempState205[1].OpenedDoor then
                    isTushitaQuestUnlocked = true
                    return
                end
            else
                if
                    enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                    or replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")
                then
                    if enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form") then
                        for loopIndex212, loopValue213 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                            local tempState214 = {}
                            tempState214[3], tempState214[1] = loopIndex212, loopValue213
                            if
                                tempState214[1].Name == "rip_indra True Form"
                                and tempState214[1].Humanoid.Health > 0
                            then
                                repeat

                                        farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
                                    end
                                    tweenTeleportTo(
                                        tempState214[1].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -30, 0),
                                        1.5
                                    )
                                    equipPreferredFarmTool()
                                until not tempState214[1].Parent or tempState214[1].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                            end
                        end
                    elseif replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form") then
                        tweenTeleportTo(
                            (replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("rip_indra True Form")).HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0)
                        )
                    end
                elseif
                    farmPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("God's Chalice")
                    or farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("God's Chalice")
                then
                    repeat
                        Utils.wt(0.1)
                        isOysterColorActivated = false
                        isHotPinkColorActivated = false
                        isReallyRedColorActivated = false
                        for loopIndex215, loopValue216 in
                            pairs(workspaceService.Map["Boat Castle"].Summoner.Circle:GetChildren())
                        do
                            local tempState217 = {}
                            tempState217[1], tempState217[2] = loopIndex215, loopValue216
                            if
                                tempState217[2].Name == "Part"
                                and (
