                        local tempState274 = {}
                        tempState274[2], tempState274[1] = loopIndex272, loopValue273
                        if
                            tempState274[1].Name == "Shanda"
                            and (tempState274[1]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and tempState274[1].Humanoid.Health > 0)
                        then
                            repeat
                                Utils.wt()
                                isSpecialRouteActive = true
                                tweenTeleportTo(tempState274[1].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0), 1.5)
                                equipPreferredFarmTool()
                                Utils.BN(tempState274[1].Name)
                            until not tempState274[1].Parent
                                or tempState274[1].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                or playerLevelValue.Value >= 91
                                or not (getgenv()).AutoFarm
                                or currentTaskName ~= nil
                        end
                    end
                else
                    tweenTeleportTo(CFrame.new(-7757, 5582, -481), 1.5)
                end
            else
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
                    "requestEntrance",
                    Vector3.new(-7894.6176757813, 5547.1416015625, -380.29119873047)
                )
            end
        elseif playerLevelValue.Value >= 71 then
            if farmPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible then
                if
                    Utils.sf(
                        farmPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text,
                        tostring(levelEnemyDisplayName)
                    )
                then
                    if Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, levelEnemyName) then
                        for loopIndex275, loopValue276 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                            local tempState277 = {}
                            tempState277[1], tempState277[2] = loopIndex275, loopValue276
                            if
                                tempState277[2].Name == levelEnemyName
                                and (
                                    tempState277[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                                    and (
                                        tempState277[2].Humanoid.Health > 0
                                        and tempState277[2]:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                    )
                                )
                            then
                                repeat
                                    Utils.wt()
                                    isSpecialRouteActive = false
                                    tweenTeleportTo(tempState277[2].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0), 1.5)
                                    if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "HasBuso") then
                                        replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
                                    end
                                    Utils.BN(tempState277[2].Name)
                                    equipPreferredFarmTool()
                                until tempState277[2].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                                    or not enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild(levelEnemyName)
                                    or not (farmPlayer.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild("Quest")).Visible
                                    or currentTaskName ~= nil
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        tweenTeleportTo(levelEnemyCFrame, 1.5)
                    end
                else
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
                end
            else
                repeat
                    Utils.wt()
                    tweenTeleportTo(levelQuestCFrame, 1.5)
                    if
                        (levelQuestCFrame.Position - farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 5
                        and (farmPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")).Health > 0
                    then
                        Utils.wt(0.5);
                        ((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")):WaitForChild("CommF_")):InvokeServer(
                            "StartQuest",
                            levelQuestName,
                            levelQuestNumber
                        )
                    end
                until (farmPlayer.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild("Quest")).Visible
                    or not (getgenv()).AutoFarm
                    or currentTaskName ~= nil
            end
        end
    elseif playerLevelValue.Value >= 2650 and Three_World then
        isSpecialRouteActive = false
        if Utils.ffc(replicatedStorage, "Cake Prince") then
            for loopIndex278, loopValue279 in pairs(replicatedStorage:GetChildren()) do
                local tempState280 = {}
                tempState280[1], tempState280[2] = loopIndex278, loopValue279
                if
                    tempState280[2].Name == "Cake Prince"
                    and (
                        tempState280[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        and (tempState280[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")).Health > 0
                    )
                then
                    tweenTeleportTo(tempState280[2].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 42, 10), 1.5)
                end
            end
        elseif Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Cake Prince") then
            for loopIndex281, loopValue282 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                local tempState283 = {}
                tempState283[1], tempState283[2] = loopIndex281, loopValue282
                if
                    tempState283[2].Name == "Cake Prince"
                    and (
                        tempState283[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        and (tempState283[2]:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")).Health > 0
                    )
                then
                    tweenTeleportTo(tempState283[2].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 42, 10), 1.5)
                end
            end
        else
            if
                tostring(
                        string.match(
                            tostring(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CakePrinceSpawner")),
                            "%d+"
                        )
                    )
                    == "nil"
                or tostring(
                        string.match(
                            tostring(replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CakePrinceSpawner")),
                            "%d+"
                        )
                    )
                    == nil
            then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("CakePrinceSpawner", true)
            end
            if
                Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Cookie Crafter")
                or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Cake Guard")
                or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Baking Staff")
                or Utils.ffc(enemiesFolder, "Head Baker")
            then
                for loopIndex284, loopValue285 in pairs(enemiesFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    local tempState286 = {}
                    tempState286[1], tempState286[2] = loopIndex284, loopValue285
                    if
                        tempState286[2].Name == "Cookie Crafter"
                        or tempState286[2].Name == "Cake Guard"
                        or tempState286[2].Name == "Baking Staff"
                        or tempState286[2].Name == "Head Baker"
                    then
                        repeat
                            Utils.wt()
                            tweenTeleportTo(tempState286[2].HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0), 1.5)
                            if not Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "HasBuso") then
                                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Buso")
                            end
                            Utils.BN(tempState286[2].Name)
                            equipPreferredFarmTool()
                        until tempState286[2].Humanoid.Health <= 0
                            or not enemiesFolder:FindFirstChild(levelEnemyName)
                            or not (farmPlayer.PlayerGui.Main:FindFirstChild("Quest")).Visible
                            or currentTaskName ~= nil
                    end
                end
            else
                tweenTeleportTo(CFrame.new(-2091.9118652344, 70.008842468262, -12142.8359375), 1.5)
            end
        end
    end
end
selectAutoFarmQuest = function()
    if not hasNoRaidFruit then
        for loopIndex287, loopValue288 in pairs(farmPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            local tempState289 = {}
            tempState289[3], tempState289[2] = loopIndex287, loopValue288
            if
                tempState289[2]:GetAttribute("OriginalName") ~= nil
                and (
                    Utils.sf(tempState289[2].Name, "Fruit")
                    and (
                        tempState289[2]:IsA("Tool")
                        and not Utils.IsInList(Utils.GetType(), tempState289[2]:GetAttribute("OriginalName"))
                    )
                )
            then
                local tempState290 = {}
                tempState290[1] = {
                    "StoreFruit",
                    tempState289[2]:GetAttribute("OriginalName"),
                    tempState289[2],
                }
                ((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")):WaitForChild("CommF_")):InvokeServer(
                    unpack(tempState290[1])
                )
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits", false)
            end
        end
        for loopIndex291, loopValue292 in pairs(farmPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
            local tempState293 = {}
            tempState293[3], tempState293[1] = loopIndex291, loopValue292
            if
                tempState293[1]:GetAttribute("OriginalName") ~= nil
                and (
                    Utils.sf(tempState293[1].Name, "Fruit")
                    and (
                        tempState293[1]:IsA("Tool")
                        and not Utils.IsInList(Utils.GetType(), tempState293[1]:GetAttribute("OriginalName"))
                    )
                )
            then
                local tempState294 = {}
                tempState294[2] = {
                    "StoreFruit",
                    tempState293[1]:GetAttribute("OriginalName"),
                    tempState293[1],
                }
                ((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")):WaitForChild("CommF_")):InvokeServer(
                    unpack(tempState294[2])
                )
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits", false)
            end
        end
    end
end
_G.Ew = true
isSecondaryToggleEnabled = true
task.spawn(function()
    while Utils.wt() do
        if _G.Ew then
            equipPreferredFarmTool()
        else
            selectAutoFarmQuest()
            if isSecondaryToggleEnabled then
                currentActionDelay = 0
            else
                currentActionDelay = 0.2
            end
        end
    end
end)

    while Utils.wt(1) do
        xpcall(function()
            if not hasBlackLeg then

                    equipPreferredFarmTool()
                    Utils.wt(0.05)
                    if
                        Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Character, "Black Leg")
                        or Utils.ffc(farmPlayer.Backpack, "Black Leg") and not hasBlackLeg

                        hasBlackLeg = true
                        Utils.Equip("Black Leg")

            end

                                hasSuperhuman = true

                                    hasSuperhumanMastery = true
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            until hasSuperhuman
            if not hasDeathStep then
                if
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDeathStep") == 1
                    or replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDeathStep") == 2
                then
                    hasDeathStep = true
                end
            end
            if hasBlackLegMastery then
                repeat
                    local tempState298 = {}
                    task.wait(currentActionDelay)

                        tempState298[1]
                        and (tempState298[1]:FindFirstChild("Level") and tempState298[1].Level.Value >= 400)
                    then
                        local tempState299 = {}
                        replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBlackLeg")
                        tempState299[1] = farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Black Leg")
                        if
                            tempState299[1]
                            and (tempState299[1]:FindFirstChild("Level") and tempState299[1].Level.Value >= 400)
                        then
                            hasBlackLegMastery = true
                        end
                    end
                until hasBlackLegMastery
            end
            if not hasDeathStepMastery then

                    equipPreferredFarmTool()
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDeathStep")

                    if

                        and (tempState300[1]:FindFirstChild("Level") and tempState300[1].Level.Value >= 400)

                    end

            if not hasElectroMastery then

            if not hasFishmanKarateMastery then

                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyDragonTalon")
                    tempState306[1] = farmPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Dragon Talon")

        end, warn)

(getgenv()).AutoFarm = true
if
    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuySharkmanKarate", true)
    ~= concatStrings({
        "I lost my house keys",
        ", could you help me ",

canStartCursedDualKatanaQuest = function()
    if not Utils.gi("Cursed Dual Katana") then
        if Utils.gi("Tushita") and Utils.gi("Yama") then
            local tempState308 = {}
            tempState308[1] = (game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")).Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
            for loopIndex309, loopValue310 in pairs(tempState308[1]) do
                local tempState311 = {}
                tempState311[3], tempState311[2] = loopIndex309, loopValue310
                if tempState311[2].Type == "Sword" then
                    if tempState311[2].Name == "Tushita" and tempState311[2].Mastery >= 400 then
                        hasTushitaMastery = true
                    elseif tempState311[2].Name == "Yama" and tempState311[2].Mastery >= 400 then
                        hasYamaMastery = true
                    end
                end
            end
            return hasTushitaMastery and hasYamaMastery
        end
    end
    return false
end
task.spawn(function()
    while Utils.wt() do
        xpcall(function()
            if (getgenv()).AutoFarm then
                local tempState312 = {}
                shouldStopFastAttack = false
                if playerLevelValue.Value >= 1500 or RainbowSaviour then
                    if
                        (((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules")):WaitForChild("Net")):WaitForChild(
                            "RF/FruitCustomizerRF"
                        )):InvokeServer({
                            ["StorageName"] = "Rainbow Saviour",
                            ["Type"] = "AuraSkin",
                            ["Context"] = "Equip",
                        }) ~= false
                    then
                        (((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules")):WaitForChild("Net")):WaitForChild(
                            "RF/FruitCustomizerRF"
                        )):InvokeServer({
                            ["StorageName"] = "Rainbow Saviour",
                            ["Type"] = "AuraSkin",
                            ["Context"] = "Equip",
                        })
                    end
                end
                tempState312[1] = false
                if
                    ((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")):WaitForChild("CommF_")):InvokeServer("Cousin", "Buy")
                    == 1
                then
                    Utils.wt(1)
                    selectAutoFarmQuest()
                else
                    for loopIndex313, loopValue314 in pairs(workspaceService:GetChildren()) do
                        local tempState315 = {}
                        tempState315[1], tempState315[3] = loopIndex313, loopValue314
                        if
                            tempState315[3]:GetAttribute("OriginalName") ~= nil
                            and (
                                Utils.sf(tempState315[3].Name, "Fruit")
                                and (
                                    tempState315[3]:IsA("Tool")
                                    and not Utils.IsInList(
                                        Utils.GetType(),
                                        tempState315[3]:GetAttribute("OriginalName")
                                    )
                                )
                            )
                        then
                            repeat
                                Utils.wt(0.1)
                                Utils.Status(" Status : TP To " .. tempState315[3].Name)
                                tweenToCFrameQuick(tempState315[3].Handle.CFrame)
                                tempState312[1] = true
                                selectAutoFarmQuest()
                            until not tempState315[3]
                                or game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(tempState315[3].Name)
                                or Utils.IsInList(Utils.GetType(), tempState315[3]:GetAttribute("OriginalName"))
                                or (tempState315[3].Handle.Position - farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 10
                                or not (getgenv()).AutoFarm
                        end
                    end
                    if not tempState312[1] then
                        local tempState316 = {}
                        if
                            Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Magma Blaster")
                            and (
                                playerLevelValue.Value >= 200
                                and (not Utils.gi("Magma Blaster") and (Old_World and Utils.CheckBoss("Magma Admiral")))
                            )
                        then
                            currentTaskName = "Magma Blaster"
                            return
                        end
                        if
                            Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Bazooka")
                            and (
                                playerLevelValue.Value >= 200
                                and (not Utils.gi("Bazooka") and (Old_World and Utils.CheckBoss("Wysper")))
                            )
                        then
                            currentTaskName = "Bazooka"
                            return
                        end
                        if
                            Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Saber")
                            and (playerLevelValue.Value >= 200 and not Utils.gi("Saber"))
                        then
                            currentTaskName = "Saber"
                            return
                        end
                        if
                            Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Shark Saw")
                            and (
                                playerLevelValue.Value >= 100
                                and (not Utils.gi("Shark Saw") and (Old_World and Utils.CheckBoss("The Saw")))
                            )
                        then
                            currentTaskName = "Shark Saw"
                            return
                        end
                        if
                            Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Wardens Sword")
                            and (
                                playerLevelValue.Value >= 100
                                and (not Utils.gi("Wardens Sword") and (Old_World and Utils.CheckBoss("Chief Warden")))
                            )
                        then
                            currentTaskName = "Wardens Sword"
                            return
                        end
                        if
                            Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Pole (1st Form)")
                            and (
                                playerLevelValue.Value >= 100
                                and (not Utils.gi("Pole (1st Form)") and (Old_World and Utils.CheckBoss("Thunder God")))
                            )
                        then
                            currentTaskName = "Pole (1st Form)"
                            return
                        end
                        if
                            ((replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")):WaitForChild("CommF_")):InvokeServer(
                                    "Alchemist",
                                    "1"
                                )

task.spawn(function()
    while Utils.wt() do
        xpcall(function()
            if currentTaskName ~= nil then
                Utils.Status(" Status : " .. currentTaskName)
            end
            if currentTaskName == "Saber" then
                local tempState430 = {}
                if not old_World then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TravelMain")
                end
                tempState430[2] = replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("ProQuestProgress")
                if tempState430[2].UsedTorch == false then
                    for loopIndex431, loopValue432 in
                        pairs((game:GetService("Workspace")).Map.Jungle.QuestPlates:GetChildren())
                    do
                        local tempState433 = {}
                        tempState433[3], tempState433[2] = loopIndex431, loopValue432
                        if
                            table.find({
                                "Plate1",
                                "Plate2",
                                "Plate3",
                                "Plate4",
                                "Plate5",
                            }, tempState433[2].Name)
                        then
                            tempState433[2].Button.CFrame = farmPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                        end
                    end
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("ProQuestProgress", "GetTorch")
                    Utils.Equip("Torch")
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("ProQuestProgress", "DestroyTorch")
                elseif tempState430[2].UsedCup == false then

idlePlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
idlePlayer = idlePlayersService.LocalPlayer
waitForCharacterRootPart = function()
    while not idlePlayer.Character or not idlePlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") do
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
    return idlePlayer.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
lastKnownPosition = (waitForCharacterRootPart()).Position
idleSeconds = 0
idleCheckSeconds = 1
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if
            currentTaskName ~= "Cursed Dual Katana"
            and (currentTaskName ~= "Evo Race V2" and (currentTaskName ~= "Evo Race V1" and not isSpecialRouteActive))
        then
            local tempState650 = {}
            task.wait(idleCheckSeconds)
            tempState650[2] = (waitForCharacterRootPart()).Position
            tempState650[1] = (tempState650[2] - lastKnownPosition).Magnitude
            if tempState650[1] <= 1 then
                idleSeconds = idleSeconds + idleCheckSeconds
                if
                    idleSeconds >= 30
                    and (
                        currentTaskName ~= "Cursed Dual Katana"
                        and (
                            currentTaskName ~= "Evo Race V2"
                            and (currentTaskName ~= "Evo Race V1" and not isSpecialRouteActive)
                        )
                    )
                then
                    Utils.HopLowServer(9)
                end
            else
                idleSeconds = 0
                lastKnownPosition = tempState650[2]
            end
        end
    end
end)

            fireproximityprompt(combatWorkspace.Map.HellDimension.Torch2.ProximityPrompt)
            fireproximityprompt(combatWorkspace.Map.HellDimension.Torch3.ProximityPrompt)
        end
    end
end)

        pcall(function()
            if playerBeliValue.Value >= 2500000 and New_World then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LegendarySwordDealer", "1")
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LegendarySwordDealer", "2")
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LegendarySwordDealer", "3")
            end
            if
                Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Kabucha") and (playerFragmentsValue.Value >= 10000 and not Utils.gi("Kabucha"))
            then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BlackbeardReward", "Slingshot", "2")
            end
            if playerBeliValue.Value >= 3000000 then
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Bisento") and not Utils.gi("Bisento") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Bisento")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Cutlass") and not Utils.gi("Cutlass") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Cutlass")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Katana") and not Utils.gi("Katana") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Katana")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Dual Katana") and not Utils.gi("Dual Katana") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Dual Katana")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Soul Cane") and not Utils.gi("Soul Cane") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Soul Cane")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Triple Katana") and not Utils.gi("Triple Katana") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Triple Katana")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Iron Mace") and not Utils.gi("Iron Mace") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Iron Mace")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Pipe") and not Utils.gi("Pipe") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Pipe")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Dual-Headed Blade") and not Utils.gi("Dual-Headed Blade") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Dual-Headed Blade")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Musket") and not Utils.gi("Musket") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Musket")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Flintlock") and not Utils.gi("Flintlock") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Flintlock")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Refined Slingshot") and not Utils.gi("Refined Slingshot") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Refined Slingshot")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Dual Flintlock") and not Utils.gi("Dual Flintlock") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Dual Flintlock")
                end
                if Utils.tf(Configs.Gun, "Cannon") and not Utils.gi("Cannon") then
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", "Cannon")
                end
            end
            if
                Utils.tf(Configs.Sword, "Midnight Blade")
                and (
                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Ectoplasm", "Check") >= 100
                    and not Utils.gi("Midnight Blade")
                )
            then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Ectoplasm", "Buy", 3)
            end
            if not hasBoughtMovementAbilities and playerLevelValue.Value >= 2000 then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyHaki", "Geppo")
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyHaki", "Soru")
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("KenTalk", "Buy")
                hasBoughtMovementAbilities = true
            end
            if not hasBoughtBusoHaki and playerLevelValue.Value >= 1000 then
                replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyHaki", "Buso")
                hasBoughtBusoHaki = true
            end
            Utils.wt(100)
        end)
    end
end)
